Create `tests/tray_update.rs`. It uses its own small loopback server, so it depends only on the update spec's documented behaviour: `MAILTRIAGE_UPDATE_URL`, the release list path, `update.json`'s shape, the JSON results and the exit codes. If auto-update's `tests/update_support` (`Sandbox`, its server, `archive`) covers the same ground, you may build these tests on it instead; keep every assertion.

```rust
#![cfg(unix)]
//! `mailtriage update` and `watch` with a `mailtriage-tray` next to the CLI,
//! against a loopback release server. The binary under test is copied into
//! a temporary directory, which is its installation directory.
use flate2::{write::GzEncoder, Compression};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use std::{
    collections::HashMap,
    fs,
    io::{BufRead, BufReader, Write},
    net::{TcpListener, TcpStream},
    os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt,
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    process::Command,
    sync::{Arc, Mutex},
    thread,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};

const RUNNING: &str = env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION");
const LIST: &str = "/repos/wir-drei-digital/mailtriage/releases?per_page=30";

/// This platform's asset suffix; the tests do nothing on other platforms.
fn platform() -> Option<&'static str> {
    if cfg!(all(target_os = "macos", target_arch = "aarch64")) {
        Some("macos-arm64")
    } else if cfg!(all(target_os = "linux", target_arch = "x86_64")) {
        Some("linux-amd64")
    } else if cfg!(all(target_os = "linux", target_arch = "aarch64")) {
        Some("linux-arm64")
    } else {
        None
    }
}

/// A loopback HTTP server: fixed routes, every requested path recorded.
struct Server {
    url: String,
    routes: Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<u8>>>>,
    hits: Arc<Mutex<Vec<String>>>,
}

impl Server {
    fn start() -> Self {
        let listener = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
        let url = format!("http://{}", listener.local_addr().unwrap());
        let routes = Arc::new(Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));
        let hits = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
        let (r, h) = (Arc::clone(&routes), Arc::clone(&hits));
        thread::spawn(move || {
            for stream in listener.incoming().flatten() {
                serve(stream, &r, &h);
            }
        });
        Self { url, routes, hits }
    }

    fn route(&self, path: &str, body: Vec<u8>) {
        self.routes.lock().unwrap().insert(path.to_owned(), body);
    }

    fn hits(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.hits.lock().unwrap().clone()
    }
}

fn serve(
    mut stream: TcpStream,
    routes: &Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<u8>>>,
    hits: &Mutex<Vec<String>>,
) {
    let mut reader = BufReader::new(stream.try_clone().unwrap());
    let mut request = String::new();
    if reader.read_line(&mut request).is_err() {
        return;
    }
    let path = request.split_whitespace().nth(1).unwrap_or("/").to_owned();
    loop {
        let mut header = String::new();
        if reader.read_line(&mut header).unwrap_or(0) == 0 || header == "\r\n" {
            break;
        }
    }
    hits.lock().unwrap().push(path.clone());
    let (status, body) = match routes.lock().unwrap().get(&path).cloned() {
        Some(body) => ("200 OK", body),
        None => ("404 Not Found", b"{}".to_vec()),
    };
    let _ = write!(
        stream,
        "HTTP/1.1 {status}\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nContent-Type: application/octet-stream\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    let _ = stream.write_all(&body);
}

/// A gzip tar holding `binary` (a script that prints `BINARY VERSION` for
/// `--version`) and `LICENSE`.
fn archive(binary: &str, version: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    let script =
        format!("#!/bin/sh\nif [ \"$1\" = --version ]; then echo '{binary} {version}'; exit 0; fi\n");
    let mut tar = tar::Builder::new(GzEncoder::new(Vec::new(), Compression::default()));
    for (name, data, mode) in [
        (binary, script.as_bytes(), 0o755),
        ("LICENSE", b"MIT\n".as_slice(), 0o644),
    ] {
        let mut header = tar::Header::new_gnu();
        header.set_size(data.len() as u64);
        header.set_mode(mode);
        header.set_cksum();
        tar.append_data(&mut header, name, data).unwrap();
    }
    tar.into_inner().unwrap().finish().unwrap()
}

/// What a test release holds.
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
struct Release {
    tray: bool,
    bad_tray_sum: bool,
}

/// Publishes `version` on `server`: the CLI archive, the tray archive when
/// asked, and `SHA256SUMS`; returns the asset listing.
fn publish(server: &Server, version: &str, release: Release, list: bool) -> Vec<Value> {
    let platform = platform().unwrap();
    let mut files = vec![(
        format!("mailtriage-v{version}-{platform}.tar.gz"),
        archive("mailtriage", version),
    )];
    if release.tray {
        files.push((
            format!("mailtriage-tray-v{version}-{platform}.tar.gz"),
            archive("mailtriage-tray", version),
        ));
    }
    let mut sums = String::new();
    for (name, data) in &files {
        let mut digest = format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(data));
        if release.bad_tray_sum && name.starts_with("mailtriage-tray-") {
            digest = "0".repeat(64);
        }
        sums += &format!("{digest}  {name}\n");
    }
    files.push(("SHA256SUMS".into(), sums.into_bytes()));
    let assets: Vec<Value> = files
        .into_iter()
        .map(|(name, data)| {
            let path = format!("/download/v{version}/{name}");
            let url = format!("{}{path}", server.url);
            let size = data.len();
            server.route(&path, data);
            json!({"name": name, "size": size, "browser_download_url": url, "url": url})
        })
        .collect();
    if list {
        server.route(
            LIST,
            json!([{
                "tag_name": format!("v{version}"),
                "name": format!("mailtriage v{version}"),
                "draft": false,
                "prerelease": false,
                "html_url": format!("{}/releases/v{version}", server.url),
                "published_at": "2026-11-02T09:00:00Z",
                "assets": assets
            }])
            .to_string()
            .into_bytes(),
        );
    }
    assets
}

/// The installation directory with the copied CLI and, optionally, a tray
/// that prints `mailtriage-tray VERSION` (or fails when `runs` is false).
struct Install {
    _dir: tempfile::TempDir,
    root: PathBuf,
    bin: PathBuf,
}

fn install(tray: Option<(&str, bool)>) -> Install {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let root = fs::canonicalize(dir.path()).unwrap();
    let bin = root.join("bin");
    for d in [&bin, &root.join("home"), &root.join("cache"), &root.join("cwd")] {
        fs::create_dir_all(d).unwrap();
    }
    fs::set_permissions(&bin, fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
    fs::copy(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_mailtriage"), bin.join("mailtriage")).unwrap();
    fs::set_permissions(bin.join("mailtriage"), fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
    if let Some((version, runs)) = tray {
        let script = if runs {
            format!("#!/bin/sh\necho 'mailtriage-tray {version}'\n")
        } else {
            "#!/bin/sh\necho 'cannot open display' >&2\nexit 1\n".to_owned()
        };
        fs::write(bin.join("mailtriage-tray"), script).unwrap();
        fs::set_permissions(bin.join("mailtriage-tray"), fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).unwrap();
    }
    Install {
        _dir: dir,
        root,
        bin,
    }
}

impl Install {
    fn command(&self, server: &Server, args: &[&str]) -> Command {
        let mut command = Command::new(self.bin.join("mailtriage"));
        command
            .args(args)
            .current_dir(self.root.join("cwd"))
            .env("HOME", self.root.join("home"))
            .env("XDG_CACHE_HOME", self.root.join("cache"))
            .env("MAILTRIAGE_UPDATE_URL", &server.url)
            .env_remove("MAILTRIAGE_CONFIG");
        command
    }

    fn run(&self, server: &Server, args: &[&str]) -> (i32, Value) {
        let out = self.command(server, args).output().unwrap();
        (
            out.status.code().unwrap(),
            serde_json::from_slice(&out.stdout).unwrap_or(Value::Null),
        )
    }

    fn cache_file(&self) -> PathBuf {
        if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
            self.root.join("home/Library/Caches/mailtriage/update.json")
        } else {
            self.root.join("cache/mailtriage/update.json")
        }
    }

    fn installs(&self) -> Value {
        serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&fs::read(self.cache_file()).unwrap()).unwrap()["installs"]
            .clone()
    }

    fn key(&self, name: &str) -> String {
        self.bin.join(name).display().to_string()
    }
}

fn version_of(path: &Path) -> String {
    String::from_utf8(Command::new(path).arg("--version").output().unwrap().stdout)
        .unwrap()
        .trim()
        .to_owned()
}

const FULL: Release = Release {
    tray: true,
    bad_tray_sum: false,
};

#[test]
fn a_tray_next_to_the_cli_is_updated() {
    if platform().is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let server = Server::start();
    publish(&server, "9.9.9", FULL, true);
    let i = install(Some(("0.1.0", true)));
    let (code, v) = i.run(&server, &["update", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{v}");
    assert_eq!(v["update"]["action"], "updated");
    let tray = &v["update"]["tray"];
    assert_eq!(
        (tray["action"].clone(), tray["from"].clone(), tray["to"].clone()),
        (json!("updated"), json!("0.1.0"), json!("9.9.9"))
    );
    assert!(tray["error"].is_null());
    assert_eq!(version_of(&i.bin.join("mailtriage-tray")), "mailtriage-tray 9.9.9");
    assert_eq!(
        version_of(&i.bin.join("mailtriage-tray.previous")),
        "mailtriage-tray 0.1.0"
    );
    let installs = i.installs();
    assert_eq!(installs[i.key("mailtriage")]["version"], "9.9.9");
    assert_eq!(installs[i.key("mailtriage-tray")]["version"], "9.9.9");
}

#[test]
fn a_tray_that_does_not_run_here_is_skipped_without_a_download() {
    if platform().is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let server = Server::start();
    publish(&server, "9.9.9", FULL, true);
    let i = install(Some(("0.1.0", false)));
    let (code, v) = i.run(&server, &["update", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{v}");
    assert_eq!(v["update"]["tray"]["action"], "skipped");
    assert!(v["update"]["tray"]["error"]
        .as_str()
        .unwrap()
        .starts_with("mailtriage-tray does not run here: "));
    assert!(server.hits().iter().all(|h| !h.contains("mailtriage-tray-v")));
    assert!(i.installs()[i.key("mailtriage-tray")].is_object());
    assert_eq!(i.installs()[i.key("mailtriage")]["version"], "9.9.9");
}

#[test]
fn a_bad_tray_checksum_fails_only_the_tray() {
    if platform().is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let server = Server::start();
    publish(
        &server,
        "9.9.9",
        Release {
            tray: true,
            bad_tray_sum: true,
        },
        true,
    );
    let i = install(Some(("0.1.0", true)));
    let (code, v) = i.run(&server, &["update", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 4, "{v}");
    assert_eq!(v["update"]["action"], "updated");
    assert_eq!(v["update"]["tray"]["action"], "failed");
    assert_eq!(version_of(&i.bin.join("mailtriage")), "mailtriage 9.9.9");
    assert_eq!(version_of(&i.bin.join("mailtriage-tray")), "mailtriage-tray 0.1.0");
    let installs = i.installs();
    assert_eq!(installs[i.key("mailtriage")]["version"], "9.9.9");
    assert!(!installs[i.key("mailtriage-tray")]["last_error"].is_null());
}

#[test]
fn a_release_without_a_tray_archive_fails_the_tray_part_only() {
    let Some(platform) = platform() else {
        return;
    };
    let server = Server::start();
    publish(
        &server,
        "9.9.9",
        Release {
            tray: false,
            bad_tray_sum: false,
        },
        true,
    );
    let i = install(Some(("0.1.0", true)));
    let (code, v) = i.run(&server, &["update", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 4, "{v}");
    assert_eq!(v["update"]["action"], "updated");
    assert_eq!(
        v["update"]["tray"]["error"],
        format!("release v9.9.9 has no tray archive for {platform}")
    );
}

#[test]
fn a_current_tray_keeps_its_own_entry_and_no_tray_means_no_tray_key() {
    if platform().is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let server = Server::start();
    publish(&server, "9.9.9", FULL, true);
    let i = install(Some(("9.9.9", true)));
    let (code, v) = i.run(&server, &["update", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{v}");
    assert_eq!(v["update"]["tray"]["action"], "current");
    assert_eq!(i.installs()[i.key("mailtriage-tray")]["version"], "9.9.9");
    assert_eq!(i.installs()[i.key("mailtriage")]["version"], "9.9.9");

    let server = Server::start();
    publish(&server, "9.9.9", FULL, true);
    let i = install(None);
    let (code, v) = i.run(&server, &["update", "--check", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{v}");
    assert!(v["update"].get("tray").is_none());
}

#[test]
fn check_reports_the_tray_without_changing_it() {
    if platform().is_none() {
        return;
    }
    let server = Server::start();
    publish(&server, "9.9.9", FULL, true);
    let i = install(Some(("0.1.0", true)));
    let (code, v) = i.run(&server, &["update", "--check", "--json"]);
    assert_eq!(code, 0, "{v}");
    assert_eq!(
        v["update"]["tray"],
        json!({"path": i.key("mailtriage-tray"), "installed": "0.1.0", "available": true})
    );
    assert_eq!(version_of(&i.bin.join("mailtriage-tray")), "mailtriage-tray 0.1.0");
    assert!(server.hits().iter().all(|h| !h.contains("/download/")));
}

/// After the CLI runs the current release, a later `watch` pass installs
/// the still-older tray once its backoff has passed, from the persisted
/// cache alone: the server serves no release list.
#[test]
fn watch_installs_an_older_tray_from_the_cache_after_its_backoff() {
    let Some(platform) = platform() else {
        return;
    };
    let server = Server::start();
    let assets = publish(&server, RUNNING, FULL, false);
    let i = install(Some(("0.0.1", true)));
    assert_eq!(i.run(&server, &["init", "--json"]).0, 0);
    let config = fs::canonicalize(i.root.join("cwd/mailtriage.json")).unwrap();
    let asset = |name: &str| {
        let a = assets.iter().find(|a| a["name"] == name).unwrap();
        json!({"name": a["name"], "url": a["browser_download_url"], "size": a["size"]})
    };
    let now = chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    let cache = json!({
        "schema_version": 1,
        "release": {
            "version": RUNNING,
            "release_url": format!("{}/releases/v{RUNNING}", server.url),
            "published_at": now,
            "archives": {
                "mailtriage": asset(&format!("mailtriage-v{RUNNING}-{platform}.tar.gz")),
                "mailtriage-tray": asset(&format!("mailtriage-tray-v{RUNNING}-{platform}.tar.gz"))
            },
            "sums": asset("SHA256SUMS")
        },
        "checked_at": now,
        "next_check_at": "2999-01-01T00:00:00Z",
        "check_failures": 0,
        "last_check_error": null,
        "configs": {config.display().to_string(): {"mode": "auto", "notified_version": null}},
        "installs": {
            i.key("mailtriage"): {"version": RUNNING, "at": now, "last_error": null, "failures": 0, "next_attempt_at": null},
            i.key("mailtriage-tray"): {
                "version": "0.0.1",
                "at": now,
                "last_error": {"at": now, "message": "an earlier failure"},
                "failures": 1,
                "next_attempt_at": "2000-01-01T00:00:00Z"
            }
        }
    });
    fs::create_dir_all(i.cache_file().parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    fs::write(i.cache_file(), cache.to_string()).unwrap();
    let mut watch = i
        .command(
            &server,
            &["watch", "--account", "work", "--interval-seconds", "1", "--json"],
        )
        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())
        .spawn()
        .unwrap();
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(30);
    while version_of(&i.bin.join("mailtriage-tray")) != format!("mailtriage-tray {RUNNING}") {
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "the tray was not installed");
        thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200));
    }
    watch.kill().unwrap();
    watch.wait().unwrap();
    assert!(server.hits().iter().all(|h| h != LIST), "{:?}", server.hits());
    assert!(i.installs()[i.key("mailtriage-tray")]["last_error"].is_null());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `cargo test --locked --workspace --test tray_update`
Expected: failures. `update.tray` is absent and `update.json` has no tray entry, while the CLI part already updates. On other platforms the tests return early.

- [ ] **Step 3: One set of service-file decoders**

The merge brings two copies of the same decoders: `service_control::{plist_arguments, unit_arguments}` (Task 2) and `update::service_files::{plist_arguments, unit_arguments}` (auto-update), with the same signatures. In `src/service_control.rs`:
- delete the two functions;
- import `crate::update::service_files::{plist_arguments, unit_arguments}`;
- keep `split_words` (still used by `exec_start_arguments`);
- in its unit test `unit_and_plist_arguments_invert_their_writers`, call the imported functions.

Run: `cargo test --locked --workspace --test service_status --test service_start_stop --lib service_control`
Expected: unchanged results. If the two decoders disagree on a case (an XML entity or a quoting rule), keep the updater's and add that case to `service_control`'s unit test.

- [ ] **Step 4: The tray component**

Add `TRAY`, the new `COMPONENTS` and `tray_path` (Interfaces above) to `src/update/mod.rs`. `release::select` already records an archive entry per component, so the cache's `release.archives` now holds `mailtriage-tray` (`null` for a release without one).

Run: `cargo test --locked --workspace --test update_check`
Expected: pass. If a test pins `archives` to the CLI alone, extend it with `"mailtriage-tray": null` (or the fixture's tray asset); that is this task's permitted edit.

- [ ] **Step 5: The tray part of `update` and `update --check`**

In `update::command::run`, after the CLI part (installed or current, under the same installation lock and before the cache is written for the last time), and only when `tray_path(cli)` is a regular file:
1. **Does it run here?** `platform::probe(&tray, TRAY)`. On `Err(cause)`:
   - report `{"action":"skipped","from":null,"to":null,"error":"mailtriage-tray does not run here: CAUSE"}`;
   - download nothing;
   - write `installs[canonical tray]` with `version: null`, `last_error: {at, message}` and `failures` unchanged (ruling 28).
2. **No tray archive.** When the release's `archives["mailtriage-tray"]` is `null`, report `failed` with `release vX.Y.Z has no tray archive for PLATFORM`, and record it like any tray failure (step 4).
3. **Install.** Otherwise run `install::install(&Job { component: TRAY, path: tray, ..the CLI job's net, release, fallback, cache and hooks }, &lock, …)`:
   - `Outcome::Installed(Installed { from, to, .. })` reports `updated` with `from` and `to`;
   - `Outcome::Current { installed }` reports `current` with `from` = `to` = installed.
4. **A tray failure** (`Err`):
   - report `failed` with the error's message;
   - leave the old tray in place (`install` never touches it before its commit point);
   - record `installs[tray].last_error` with the update spec's backoff (`failures + 1`, `next_attempt_at` 1 h doubling up to 24 h);
   - add top-level `"partial": true`.

   The CLI part is never undone.
5. Put the object at `update.tray` in the result. Without a tray file there is no `tray` key.

In `update --check` (`run(true, …)`) and in `report::update_block`, add `"tray": {"path": tray, "installed": probe(..).ok().map(to_string), "available": installed < release}` when the file exists. `available` is false when `installed` is `null` or there is no release. The CLI's `available` keeps meaning the CLI alone.

- [ ] **Step 6: The tray in `watch`'s background install**

In `WatchUpdates::before_pass`, the auto-install decision runs per component, CLI first, then the tray when `tray_path` exists:
- each has its own `installs[path]` entry, reservation and `next_attempt_at`;
- each installs from the cached release only (`archives["mailtriage-tray"]`, `sums`), never refreshing for it;
- a tray that does not run here is skipped, as in Step 5;
- a tray failure prints one `update` error event and never fails the pass;
- the CLI's restart rule is unchanged, so after a re-exec onto a current CLI the older tray installs at a later pass.

- [ ] **Step 7: Run the tests to verify they pass**

Run: `cargo test --locked --workspace --test tray_update && cargo test --locked --workspace`
Expected: 7 passed in `tray_update` (on macOS arm64 and Linux amd64/arm64); the updater's own tests unchanged.

- [ ] **Step 8: Publish the tray archives**

`build.yml` already packages and uploads them as `tray-<platform>` (Task 4). Apply to the merged `release.yml` (shown against today's; auto-update's concurrency and Latest changes stay):

```diff
diff --git a/.github/workflows/release.yml b/.github/workflows/release.yml
index 2d0362a..3402058 100644
--- a/.github/workflows/release.yml
+++ b/.github/workflows/release.yml
@@ -81,14 +81,22 @@ jobs:
           pattern: mailtriage-*
           merge-multiple: true
           path: dist
+      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v8.0.1
+        with:
+          pattern: tray-*
+          merge-multiple: true
+          path: dist
       - name: Check downloaded assets
         run: |
           set -euo pipefail
           cd dist
           archives=(*.tar.gz)
           manifests=(*.tar.gz.sha256)
-          test "${#archives[@]}" -eq 3
-          test "${#manifests[@]}" -eq 3
+          trays=(mailtriage-tray-v*.tar.gz)
+          # Three CLI and three tray archives, each with its checksum file.
+          test "${#archives[@]}" -eq 6
+          test "${#manifests[@]}" -eq 6
+          test "${#trays[@]}" -eq 3
           cat "${manifests[@]}" > SHA256SUMS
           sha256sum --check SHA256SUMS
       - name: Publish GitHub Release
```

`SHA256SUMS` is built from all six `.sha256` files, so it lists the tray archives too.

- [ ] **Step 9: Document it**

- `docs/development/releases.md`:
  - "Each release contains" gains `mailtriage-tray-vVERSION-linux-amd64.tar.gz`, `…-linux-arm64.tar.gz` and `…-macos-arm64.tar.gz`, each with `mailtriage-tray` and `LICENSE`, plus their `.sha256` files, all in `SHA256SUMS`.
  - The CLI archives stay free of GUI libraries.
  - "Publish a version" gains: bump `tray/Cargo.toml` to the same version; CI refuses a mismatch.
- `docs/guide.md`, "Updates" (from auto-update):
  - a `mailtriage-tray` next to the CLI is updated in the same run, after the CLI;
  - a tray that does not run on the host is skipped;
  - `update` exits 4 when only the tray part failed;
  - the `tray` objects in `update`, `update --check` and `service status`.
- `docs/agents/index.md`, the updates paragraph: exit 4 from `update` means the CLI is current or updated and the tray failed; report `update.tray.error` to the user.

- [ ] **Step 10: Full check and commit**

Run: `cargo fmt --all --check && cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings && cargo test --workspace --locked`

```bash
git add src tests/tray_update.rs .github/workflows/release.yml docs/development/releases.md docs/guide.md docs/agents/index.md
git commit -m "Update mailtriage-tray next to the CLI and publish its archives
```

---

## Human gate

Before relying on the tray, a person runs "Tray app on a real desktop (human check)" from `docs/development/verification.md` (Task 9) on:
- macOS in light and dark mode;
- Ubuntu GNOME with the AppIndicator extension;
- KDE Plasma.

They also run the VoiceOver and Orca checks. The automated tests use a fake `mailtriage`, fake `launchctl`/`systemctl`, a fake `/proc` and a headless window; none of them shows the real menu bar, a StatusNotifier host or a screen reader.
